- **6b-ii — Users** (D-D): every user of the deployment — the login, "you", the badges (owner, disabled, must change password, seeded default), how they sign in, their organisations, their last sign-in; never an email. New local user asks for no password: the browser draws a temporary one (`temporaryPassword`: 20 symbols of `PASSWORD_ALPHABET`, from Web Crypto, in groups of four — 24 characters), sends it with the create, then sets it again (`POST /api/admin/users/:id/password`) so the account must change it at first sign-in. The dialog shows it once; it is never in the status line, a title, an aria-label, a live region, the browser's storage or the console; Close, leaving, a sign-in and `pagehide` let go of it. Manage…: Reset password (a new one, shown once), Disable / Enable, Sign out everywhere, Make / Revoke owner, each confirmed, each status from the server's answer; the last enabled owner's Disable and Revoke owner and one's own Reset are drawn `aria-disabled` with the server's rule. Disabling or signing out oneself clears this browser's traces of that login (the MCP URLs and endpoints, the deploy profiles, every snapshot, the recent services) before the sentence and Go to sign-in. A server signing in through OIDC or a proxy says, before Create, that a local user cannot sign in there; a reset there, or on a server without sign-in, says so and what it still does, and Enable… and Sign out everywhere… say how the user signs in here (`userSignIn`: a local user with their password only under local sign-in; an IdP user — an OIDC issuer's or a reverse proxy's — only through their own provider, never with a password), its status agreeing.
- **6b-ii — the first user on a server without sign-in** (D-E): at `127.0.0.1` under `OBSERVOGRAM_AUTH=off`, New local user creates the owner the create arms sign-in for, makes no second call, and reads `/auth/me` to say which: sign-in is on now, or the user signs in once the server starts without `OBSERVOGRAM_AUTH=off` — the password not forced to change, and the sentence says so. `docs/STORE_PLAN.md`'s first-local-user rule names that door.
- **6b-ii — Organisations**: every org, removed ones greyed (`a slug is never reused`); New organisation (the owner its first admin; Take over an existing directory sends `adopt`) and Switch to it, which reloads into its Settings; Remove asks for the id typed, says what cannot be undone (no route restores it, its members lose access, its files stay), shows the server's note as served, and removing the org this browser is in reloads into the next. The default org's Remove is `aria-disabled` with its reason.
- **6b-ii — Act in an organisation** (D-M): an owner's org rows offer Act in <id>, which reloads this browser into that org's members — the rescue for an org whose last admin left or was disabled. The head reads `you are an owner acting in <id> — not a member`; for an owner the ORG chip's entry reads `<name> — acting as owner` (a member's view of the chip is unchanged); the chip leaves it; the saved org remembers the login that chose it (`studioOrgBy.v1`, localStorage — cleared at sign-out, the account menu's or one's own disable or sign out everywhere from Users, with that login's other keys; the org itself, `studioOrg.v1`, stays), so on a shared browser an owner never boots into an org another login chose; an acting org removed meanwhile is left once at the next boot (`studioActingRecovery.v1`, sessionStorage) for the owner's first membership.
- **6b-ii — the join role**: the sign-in mode it applies to first (`Sign-in: local users. The join role applies to IdP users once OIDC is configured: none.`; on a server without sign-in, `This server runs without sign-in (OBSERVOGRAM_AUTH=off). The join role applies to IdP users once it restarts without OBSERVOGRAM_AUTH=off, with OIDC configured: none.` — OBSERVOGRAM_AUTH=off beats an OIDC issuer, so configuring one alone changes nothing there); viewer, operator, admin or no automatic join; admin needs the box ticked, and only that body carries `confirm` (`joinRoleBody`). **Server text** (D-F): the join-role 409 no longer names a roadmap slice — `its name, its members and its MCP endpoints`.
- **6b-ii — Tests**: 1359 → 1390 tests, from develop's total once rebadge batch 4 and the malformed-JSON follow-up had merged — `tools/test-settings-model.mjs` (users, the temporary password, the organisations, the join role, acting in an org; from the review fixes, the rank-lost confirm test, the reset where a local user cannot sign in, `userSignIn` — how each kind of user signs in under each sign-in mode —, Enable… under every mode and Sign out everywhere… under every mode), `server/test-settings-studio.mjs` (from the review fixes, the shared-browser case — an owner signing in after another login chose an org she is not in, and the sign-out leaving her login nowhere —, the long one-org label case, the reverse-proxy reset case, the rank-lost focus case — a confirm step refused by a lost rank keeps the focus in its dialog and announces the 403 — and the reverse-proxy Enable and Sign out case: local lou cannot sign in here, quin through the proxy; the owner block: a temporary password shown once and nowhere else — both storages, every title and aria-label, every live region, the console — gone at `pagehide`, after a navigation away and back and after Close, changed at nina's first sign-in, a reset refusing her old flow; the last-owner rule; an owner rescuing an org; an org created, switched to and removed; the join role's bodies; signing herself out everywhere; the first user on a server without sign-in), `server/test-identity-api.mjs` and `server/test-store.mjs` (the join-role text), `server/test-authz.mjs` (the "is next" test's terminal clause: once the build status says every slice of §7 is built, neither `docs/STORE_PLAN.md` nor `docs/HANDOVER.md` may say a slice is next — D-G; the sign-out clean-up clears `studioOrgBy.v1` when it names that login). No vendored module changed.
- **6b-ii — Docs**: README (Roles; "In the studio: Settings → Users, Organisations and Join role"), `docs/USER_JOURNEY.md` ("Settings"), `docs/STORE_PLAN.md` (slice 6b built; every slice of §7 is built), `docs/HANDOVER.md` (every STORE_PLAN slice is built; the studio conventions), `docs/UI_CONVENTIONS.md` (`copySecret`, `signIn`, `switchTo`), `docs/UPDATE_JOURNEY.md`, `.github/copilot-instructions.md` (a temporary password shown once).

### STORE_PLAN slice 6a — services as the axis
The studio's home is the org's services table and a service page is the second level under it; Discover · Diagnose · Remediate · Build open from a record bound to a service and an environment. Branch `codex/services-axis`; design decisions D-A (one PR), D-B (the editor as the droppable tail), D2, D3, D4, D6, D7, D9, D10, D11 taken as ratified.
- **The Services home** (`studio/services-view.mjs` `renderServicesHome`, the pure models in `studio/services-model.mjs`, the loaders in `studio/services-api.mjs`): one card per record from `GET /api/services` — the tier (`graded by the pack` for `null`), the owners, the packs linked, the environments each with the latest verdict of the newest primary pack graded at the record's tier (`GET /api/packs/:id/conformance?env=` — a base grade named as such where the pack declares no overlay, never that environment's pass; a report resting on placeholders never plain green; "no verdict yet" said, never a zero); catalogue packs listed apart; the pack-derived tiles only where the table is unavailable (the static bundle, a table that could not be read — the status says so and offers a retry) or for a registered pack whose service row was deleted (its tile beside the cards, or under an empty sentence that says so — a pack `GET /api/packs` lists is never unreachable from the home). A card opens the service page.
- **The service page** (`mode: 'service'`): the environments as tabs with their MCP endpoint as `{ name, origin }` and their verdict, the packs linked with the newest primary marked current (one resolver — `packForService` — for the card, the page, the header SERVICE selector and the derived tile), and the four actions opening the workspace bound to the service and the environment (the SERVICE chip is a button back to the page). The header SERVICE selector reads the table through the same resolver; a record without a pack opens its page and Discover says, worded for the rank, who registers one.
- **Build writes the service row**: after the register links the pack by slug and creates its environment, the tier and owners typed on DEFINE go onto the record where it has none (`PATCH /api/services/:id`, one `service.update` row) — a tier a person set is never overwritten and the mismatch with the pack's own tier is shown; a pack whose service name yields another slug registers a new service and the origin is left alone, and DEFINE says so first (`buildDefineOriginNote`, with a one-click `use <name>` while the origin's name still yields its slug); a table that failed to refresh leaves the row unchecked and says so; a viewer's hand-off says why the row is not theirs.
- **The service record editor** (D-B): name, owners, tier and description from the page over `PATCH /api/services/:id`, for operators; the status line prints the server's `changed` or "Nothing changed."; the slug note under the name (a slug is fixed); Save is `aria-disabled` while a save is pending and never `disabled`; a 401/403 downgrades the affordances while the dialog keeps the server's sentence; the verdict is re-read after a tier save.
- **Affordances from the effective role** (`GET /api/orgs`): a viewer's Build card, the home's three source buttons, the page's Build and Edit are drawn `aria-disabled` with the reason (`needs the operator role in <org> — yours is viewer`), never as usable; a browser beside `OBSERVOGRAM_API_TOKEN` without sign-in is a viewer with the token reason (read-only in the browser, D10). A signed-in user in no org sees the server's refusal (`403: no org membership — ask an admin to add you`) and the account menu, not the API-unreachable screen.
- **One org switcher** (D6): the chip in the OBSERVA bar keeps it (`.observa-org-select`, the list from `/auth/me`, the role and the name from `GET /api/orgs` — D7), the context bar's `#hdr-org` copy is gone; the chip's title names the effective role; no colour literal left in the org chip rules.
- **The studio state is one org's**: `studioState.v2:<login>:<org>` and `studioRecentServices:<org>`, adopted once from the old keys and cleared at sign-out — a DEFINE draft never outlives the org it came from.
- **Static bundle** (`studio/static-backend.mjs`): `GET /api/services`, `GET|PATCH /api/services/:id` answer `501 Services needs the <product> server…` and `GET /api/orgs` `501 Organisations needs …`; the home falls back to the derived tiles without a `console.error` (T7 unchanged).
- **CSS and the AA scan**: the `.svc-*` rules moved under a `==== Services` marker in `studio/app.css` the scan in `tools/test-build-model.mjs` reads; `.svc-gate-meta` and `.svc-gate-eyebrow` are `--ink-3` (the scan records the dark `--ink-4` ratio it refuses); the zone's block comment records what `ux.css` / `reskin.css` override, and the test asserts the new prefixes are free of it.
- **Tests**: `tools/test-services-model.mjs` (new), `server/test-services-studio.mjs` (new — the journey in headless Chromium through `OBSERVOGRAM_PLAYWRIGHT`, skipped without it, `OBSERVOGRAM_SERVICES_SMOKE=require` fails instead; the knob joins `server/fixtures/serve-child.mjs` STRIP), `server/test-services-api.mjs` (Build's hand-off over HTTP: a second register is a second primary, a renamed record is never patched), `tools/test-build-model.mjs` (the scan extension, the override assertion, the hand-off and origin-note models), `tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` (T6: `Services`, `Organisations`), `tools/test-studio-layout.mjs` (the editor exemptions). No vendored module changed.
- **Docs**: README (Main Journey, the services section's "In the studio", the static bundle sentence), `docs/USER_JOURNEY.md` (invariant #9, "Home and the service page"), `docs/BUILD_JOURNEY.md` (the flow, the hand-off, Build from a service page), `docs/STORE_PLAN.md` (slice 6a built; slice 6 (6b, Settings) is next), `docs/HANDOVER.md`, `docs/UI_CONVENTIONS.md` (`host.services`), `.github/copilot-instructions.md` (the studio conventions line).

### Rebadge batch 2, B3.5: the service audit report
One exportable report per pack — HTML and JSON — that reads, in order, the maturity rubric's grade, the placeholders, a reviewer's verdicts, the service's waivers, the coverage by family, the goes-blind risks and the declared response path. Every section quotes an engine this repository already ships; nothing here grades, scores or classifies on its own, and a verdict never feeds the conformance numbers (the engine's numbers headline, a waivers overlay's `effective` sits beside them).
- **`tools/lib/audit-report.mjs`** (new; listed — `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json` 53 → 54 modules; imports `artefact-classify.mjs`, `blast-radius.mjs`, `brand.mjs`, `pack-conformance.mjs`, `remediation-flow.mjs`; the traceability graph arrives as a shape, so the PromQL parser is never imported): `buildAuditReport(input)` → the document (`reportVersion`, `generator`, `generatedAt`, `pack`, `tier`, `conformance`, `placeholders`, `assessments`, `waivers`, `coverage`, `goesBlind`, `responsePath` — `docs/ADAPTER.md` "The service audit report"), the section builders `splitClauses`, `placeholdersOf`, `assessmentSummary`, `waiverSummary`, `coverageByFamily` (`required` = named by a rubric clause that applies at the graded tier; `CLAUSE_FAMILIES` over every rubric id, `[]` for the referential L2X clause), `goesBlindRisks` (the top N by what goes blind, the count of nodes whose loss would blind an SLO), `responsePath` (B3.3's model, `compared: false` by construction), `flattenArtefacts` (the walk `server/verdict-admin.mjs` and the static bundle share), `renderAuditReportHtml(report, { brand, styles })` (one document over `studio/design-tokens.css` + `design-kit.css` and the `.ar-*` zone, the brand's chrome and tokens, no script, every value escaped, a `</style` stylesheet refused), `auditReportFilename`. Honest texts: a source not given reads "not recorded by this build", an empty one "none recorded". Deterministic: no clock read, `generatedAt` is the caller's, every list sorted by a stated key.
- **CLI** `tools/audit-report.mjs` / `packc audit-report <pack> [--env <name>] [--format json|html|both] [--out <path>] [--brand <file.json>] [--taxonomy <file.json>] [--top <n>] [--generated-at <iso> | --no-timestamp] [--verdicts <file.json>] [--waivers <file.json>] [--schema <file>]` (`npm run audit-report`): the schema check first, the pack graded as declared or for one environment, the graph built here (the CLI has the parser), the design tokens and kit read from `studio/`; `--brand` is the only brand it reads (never `OBSERVOGRAM_BRAND_*` — a report is reproducible from its arguments), `--no-timestamp` makes the bytes reproducible, `--verdicts` / `--waivers` feed the two sections the CLI has no store for (else "not recorded by this build"). Exit 0 written · 1 an unreadable, layered or invalid pack · 2 usage (an undeclared `--env`, an unreadable flag file, an invalid stamp named).
- **Server** (`server/routes/audit-report.mjs`, mounted after the waivers API): `GET /api/packs/:id/audit-report?format=json|html&top=&download=1&env=` (viewer; no-store; the conformance section is the ONE `conformanceReportFor()` body — the route and `/conformance` can never grade one pack differently — the verdict rows `verdictsDocument`'s views and the waiver rows `listWaiverViews`' over the pack's primary service, mapped field by field with the one `now` the conformance overlay used; the HTML branded like the shell over the studio's design tokens and kit, read at the first request; 400 names a bad `format` or `top`, 404 `{ error: 'unknown pack: <id>' }`) and the reserved **`GET /api/packs/:id/placeholders`** (viewer; `packConformance(overlaid)` bare, `?env=`). `conformanceReportFor` takes an optional `{ now }` (its default the clock, as before — `/conformance` is byte-identical). Route-table rows, `test-authz` classes and probes, `test-tenancy` rows, README API Surface rows; `server/test-audit-report-api.mjs` (new, 5).
- **Static bundle** (`studio/static-backend.mjs`): `GET /api/packs/:id/placeholders` is answered in the browser (the zero-store engine — T5 parity with a running server, no-store); `GET /api/packs/:id/audit-report` is `501 Audit report needs the <product> server…` (PACK_FEATURES; its goes-blind section is the blast radius over the traceability graph, whose PromQL parser the bundle cannot inline — the Compare blocker, `docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §10; follow-up `B3.5-bundle-audit-report`). **The declared default-output change**: the shim's static import of `tools/lib/pack-conformance.mjs` adds it (and `waivers.mjs`, already-present `conformance.mjs`) to the bundle's import map, so an unconfigured bundle's bytes change (B1's same-tree inert proof T4b holds; the bundle is not golden-gated); `tools/test-pack-conformance.mjs`'s inert pin is restated: exactly the two placeholder routes import the engine.
- **Studio** (`studio/conformance-view.mjs`; the `.conf-exports` row in `app.css`): the Conformance view gains, under the decision header, two download anchors — the audit report as HTML and as JSON for the focused pack and environment, org-scoped through `orgQuery` like every navigation. **A declared DOM change** on every Conformance render with a focused pack id (the headless capture `tools/fixtures/golden/conformance-view/payment-service.bare.html` renders with none and is byte-identical). In the static bundle the shim's capture-phase click handler answers a download anchor on one of its routes (a navigation never reaches a fetch wrapper, and a static host would 404 and cancel the download without a word): the anchors show the shim's `Audit report needs the <product> server…` sentence in the notice row, no request leaves the page (T7 asserts it; `B3.5-bundle-audit-report`).
- **Goldens** `tools/fixtures/golden/audit-report/{payment-service,http-service.tier-3,edge-hostile-names,payment-service.branded}.audit-report.golden.{json,html}` (unstamped; `npm run test:golden:audit-report[:update]`), pinned by `tools/test-audit-report.mjs` (new, 13 tests at its first commit; in `npm test` and `npm run test:audit-report`).
- **Docs**: `docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §14 `service-audit-report` (status, seams, follow-ups `B3.5-bundle-audit-report`, `B3.5-export-zip`, `B3.5-dark-print`), §10 and §11; `docs/ADAPTER.md` "Artefact addresses" and "The service audit report" (the document shape); README "Export A Service Audit Report", API Surface rows, Repository Map; `docs/CONFORMANCE.md`, `docs/UI_CONVENTIONS.md`, `docs/VENDORING.md`; the batch's delivery report `docs/DELIVERY-GAP-BATCH2.md`, guarded by `tools/test-doc-test-totals.mjs`.
- **Tests**: 975 → 998 tests — `tools/test-audit-report.mjs` 18 (the model, the goldens, the CLI, the Conformance view's anchors) and `server/test-audit-report-api.mjs` 5.

### Rebadge batch 2, B3.1: verdicts — a reviewer's per-artefact trust record
A verdict is a reviewer's `trusted | suspect | failed` record, with a reason, the actor and the time, on one artefact (the adapter's positional id, frozen within a content-hash pack id) of one registered pack; `unreviewed` is the absence of a record. A trust record, never a scorer input: nothing sums verdicts into the conformance score or the diagnostic grade (`docs/ADAPTER.md` "Verdicts — a reviewer's record per artefact"; README "Record Verdicts"). Inert without a record: every GET answers its empty document, a catalogue pack by construction.
- **API** (`server/routes/verdicts.mjs` over `server/verdict-admin.mjs`, the rules SQL-free as `service-admin.mjs` is): `GET /api/packs/:id/verdicts` (viewer; `{ ok, pack, verdicts[], summary }`, `?env=` ignored), `PUT /api/packs/:id/verdicts/:artefact` (operator; `{ status, reason? }` → `{ ok, verdict, changed }`; the same status and reason again writes no row; audit `verdict.set`), `DELETE …/verdicts/:artefact` (operator; `{ ok, cleared }`; audit `verdict.clear`). Refusals name a way out: 400 the status vocabulary / the reason length / a malformed id, 404 an unknown pack (`{ ok: false, error: 'unknown pack: <id>' }`, the services shape) or artefact, 409 a catalogue pack. The artefact index walks the adapted pack as the Discover board does (L1, L2, L2X, L3, L4 policy → alerting → healing, L5, GOV), keys every artefact by its behavioural identity (`identityKeyOf`, `#NN`-suffixed within a colliding group) and hashes its contract.
- **Studio** (`studio/verdict-html.mjs`, pure — the badge, the chip, the Refine control's Verdict filter and its predicate, importing `util.mjs` and `ux-kit.mjs` only; `studio/verdicts.mjs` — the per-pack load into `state.verdicts`, never persisted, never throwing; the drawer's Verdict section): the Discover board draws a verdict as the last child of an item, the rows (details and tiles) as a fifth status chip inside `.dv-row-status` (`STATUS_PROPERTIES.verdict` in `studio/ux-kit.mjs`: trusted ok · suspect warn · failed fail · unreviewed neutral, the diamond its shape), the Refine control offers a Verdict facet only when the pack has one, and the drawer records one — the form for operators and admins (and everyone in the open postures; beside `OBSERVOGRAM_API_TOKEN` without sign-in the browser is a viewer, read once from `GET /api/orgs`'s `effectiveRole`, and the note names the bearer route for CI instead of a Save the API answers 401) on a registered pack, the record alone otherwise, and a note naming the way (upload it) for a catalogue pack. **Inert**: with no verdict nothing renders — `tools/test-golden-board.mjs`'s 24 goldens are byte-identical (its entries carry no verdict), `tools/test-discover-rows.mjs` pins the rows with `verdict: null`. The default-output change: the single-pack drawer gains a Verdict section (read-only for viewers).
- **Static bundle** (`studio/static-backend.mjs`, source changed — the unconfigured bundle's bytes change in that module and the import map, B1's same-tree inert proof T4b holds): `GET /api/packs/:id/verdicts` answers the empty document (a bundled pack is never registered, so it is the server's own answer; `tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` T5 compares both packs), `PUT` / `DELETE …/verdicts/:artefact` answer `501 Verdicts needs the <product> server …` (T6).
- **The carry** (`server/pack-registry.mjs` `registerPack`): a re-upload under the same label with changed content (the quick-start dedup, `pack.replace`) carries each verdict of the replaced pack onto the new pack's artefact with the same behavioural identity key — the reviewer, the reason and the time travel, `carriedFrom` names the old pack — and drops the rest; one `verdict.carry` audit row on the new pack (`{ from, kept, dropped, droppedCount }`) between `pack.register` and the link rows, listed for every register route (`PACK_REGISTER`). A replaced pack without verdicts plans nothing, adapts nothing and writes nothing — its audit rows are exactly what they were. An eviction and the rehydrate's prune carry nothing (the cascade drops the rows).
- **Export** (`server/index.mjs` `GET /api/packs/:id/export.zip`): `verdicts.json` — the GET document — after the compiled artefacts, only while the pack has a verdict; `X-Bundle-Files` counts it. A catalogue pack and a pack nobody reviewed export exactly what they did (the static bundle's ZIP parity, T5, holds without a shim change).
- **Deferred by name** (`docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §14): `verdicts-service-scope`, `verdicts-bundle-bake`, `verdicts-cli`, `verdicts-on-adapter-upgrade`.
- **Tests**: `server/test-verdict-admin.mjs` (new, 9), `server/test-verdicts-api.mjs` (new, 9) and `tools/test-verdicts-panel.mjs` (new, 4: who sees the form in the token-only, open, bundle and identity postures, headless), all in `npm test`; `server/test-authz.mjs` (three route rows in EXPECTED_CLASS and PROBES), `server/test-tenancy.mjs` (three ORG_SCOPED rows; alice records a verdict in her org), `server/test-store-guards.mjs` (SQL_FREE gains `verdict-admin.mjs`), `server/test-smoke.mjs` (the empty document for `payment-service`), `tools/test-ux-kit.mjs` (the fifth property), `tools/test-discover-rows.mjs` (three: purity and null byte-identity, the badge and chip placement, the filter), `tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` (T5, T6). With the schema v2 entry below: Suite total 885 → 911.

### Rebadge batch 2, B3.2: waivers
A waiver is a time-boxed, reasoned suppression of a conformance finding — one rubric clause, optionally one canonical symbol of it (`slos.<id>`, `slis.<id>`: the adapter's `defines` vocabulary, never a JSONPath) — recorded on the service record (the API) or carried in a sidecar file (`packc conformance --waivers`). A waiver never rewrites the rubric: the report keeps the engine's numbers and gains a `waivers` block with the `effective` numbers beside them. Inert without a waiver: `/conformance` is the same object, the CLI's output byte-identical.
- **`tools/lib/conformance.mjs`** gains `SUBJECT_CLAUSES` (the four per-item clauses: `L1.MUST.sli_covered_by_slo`, `L3.MUST.recording_rule_per_slo`, `L4.MUST.multi_window_burn_rate`, `L5.MUST.tier1_chaos_for_each_slo`) and `clauseSubjects(clauseId, canonical)` → the FAILING subjects of such a clause as canonical symbols (`[]` when it passes, `null` for a whole-pack clause); the L3 predicate is one private `ruleCovers` behind both the evaluator and the subjects, so subjects ≡ verdict by construction (`tools/test-waivers.mjs` proves it over the whole catalogue). `RUBRIC` and `evaluateConformance` are untouched; `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json` regenerated (the module's exports grew).
- **`tools/lib/waivers.mjs`** (new, browser-safe, vendorable — listed in `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json`; imports `conformance.mjs` only): the waiver object (`normalizeWaiver` — `{ id, artefactId, ruleId, reason, expiresAt, author, createdAt, revokedAt, revokedBy, revokeReason }`, every text one line, `MAX_REASON` 2000 / `MAX_TEXT` 200), the computed states (`waiverState` → `active | expired | revoked`, `expiresInDays`, `waiverView`), the matching (`matchesFinding`: the clause agrees; a pack-level waiver covers every subject, a scoped one its own and beats the pack-level; the newest among equals), the findings seam (`applyWaiversToFindings(findings, waivers, { now })` → `{ findings[…status: failing | waived | expired, waiver], counts, unused }` — the shape `docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §11 reserved for the placeholder rows), the conformance overlay (`applyWaiversToConformance(report, waivers, { now, canonical })` → the SAME object without an open waiver, else `{ …report, waivers: { counts, clauses: { [id]: { status: waived | partial | expired, waivers[], subjects } }, effective: { conformant, must, should, scorePercent, mustPercent, byDimension }, unused } }` — the engine's numbers untouched) and the sidecar reader (`readWaiverFile` — `{ version: 1, waivers: [...] }`, the object the API serves per waiver). `tools/test-waivers.mjs` grows by seven.
- **API** (`server/routes/waivers.mjs` over `server/waiver-admin.mjs`, the rules SQL-free as `service-admin.mjs` is; the SQL in `server/store/waivers.mjs`): waivers live on the **service record** a pack is primarily linked to (a re-upload keeps them; a catalogue pack has no service and no waiver). `GET /api/services/:id/waivers` (viewer; `{ ok, service, waivers[], counts: { active, expired, revoked } }`, newest first, history included), `POST /api/services/:id/waivers` (operator; `{ ruleId, artefactId?, reason, expiresAt }` → 201 `{ ok, waiver }`; the author is the caller, the body's `author` / `createdAt` ignored; one active waiver per `(ruleId, artefactId)`, 409 naming it; audit `waiver.create`), `POST /api/waivers/:id/revoke` (operator; `{ reason? }` → `{ ok, waiver }`, the row kept as history; a second revoke 409; audit `waiver.revoke`). Refusals name a way out: 400 a `ruleId` outside the rubric (`GET /api/maturity-rubric`), an `artefactId` that is not `slis.<id>` / `slos.<id>` or that names a whole-pack clause (the four per-item clauses listed), a reason that is not one line of 1–2000 characters, an expiry not after now or more than 366 days ahead; 404 `no service <id>` / `no waiver <id>`. **The overlay**: `GET /api/packs/:id/conformance` is built by `conformanceReportFor` as before and, while the pack's service holds an open (unrevoked) waiver, gains a `waivers` block — `{ service: { id, slug }, counts, clauses, effective, unused }` (`docs/ADAPTER.md` "Waivers — a service record's suppression of a finding"); without one the body is the same object, byte for byte (`server/test-waivers-api.mjs` captures it before any waiver and matches it again after every waiver is revoked). `/api/validate` and the library routes keep the bare report. **Default-output change**: `DELETE /api/services/:id` answers `waivers: n` beside `environments` and `packLinks`, and its `service.delete` audit detail gains the same count (the rows cascade with the service). **Static bundle** (`studio/static-backend.mjs`): the three waiver routes answer `501 Waivers needs the <product> server…` — `FEATURES` gains the `/api/waivers` prefix and `featureOf` a regex for `/api/services/:id/waivers` (a bundled pack has no service record; `tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` T6).
- **Tests**: `server/test-waivers-api.mjs` (new, 9; in `npm test`); `server/test-authz.mjs` (three route rows in EXPECTED_CLASS and PROBES), `server/test-tenancy.mjs` (three ORG_SCOPED rows; alice records a waiver in her org), `server/test-store-guards.mjs` (SQL_FREE gains `waiver-admin.mjs`), the `waivers: 0` re-pins of `server/test-services-api.mjs` and `server/test-service-admin.mjs`.
- **CLI** — `tools/pack-conformance.mjs` gains `[--waivers <file>]` (`packc conformance … --waivers waivers.json`; README "Report Placeholders"): the file (`readWaiverFile`, read before any pack; unreadable → exit 2 `--waivers <path>: <reason>`) is applied to every pack. **`tools/lib/pack-conformance.mjs`** honours the reserved `opts.waivers` (exports unchanged, imports changed: it now imports `waivers.mjs` — `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json` regenerated, the `docs/VENDORING.md` row says so): every row is a finding `{ ruleId: row.rule, subject: row.symbol }`; a row an active waiver covers keeps its state and gains `waived: { id, reason, expiresAt, author }`, a lapsed one `lapsed`; the report gains the `waived` partition, `counts.byState.waived`, `counts.waivers` and `unusedWaivers` — the seam `docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §11 reserved. The rubric line reads `MUST a/b (+w waived) · score n% (m% with waivers)` (each note only when it says something; a waiver matching no failing clause is counted as such) and `--json`'s `rubric` gains `waivers` (the overlay's block) only when the service's — here the file's — open waivers produced one; `--strict` fails on unwaived rows alone. Without `--waivers` stdout and `--json` are byte-identical to before (no new key, no changed line — pinned). `tools/test-pack-conformance.mjs` grows by two (the `--waivers` cases and the engine's partition; the flag pin covers usage, the `packc` help line, the README synopsis and this entry).
- **Studio** (`studio/conformance-view.mjs`; `studio/ux-kit.mjs` assessment vocabulary gains `waived`, tone `info`): when the report carries a `waivers` block, a failing clause the block marks `waived` leaves Blocking for a **Waived requirements** group (its nav item, a `Waived` measure with the effective MUST count and the waivers lapsing within 30 days, a Scoring-rules bullet with the effective numbers; each row quotes who waived it until when and why, the covered symbols, and "Before it lapses" as its fix), a blocking clause whose waiver is `partial` or `expired` keeps its place with a caveat naming the remaining subjects or the lapsed waiver; an active waiver that matches no failing clause (the block's `unused`: the clause passes, or the symbol is not one the pack defines — a typo, or an SLO renamed since) is listed in a **Waivers that match nothing** group under Waived (its nav item; `· n waiver(s) match nothing` on the `Waived` measure), each with its clause, symbol, author, expiry and reason, as the CLI's `n waiver(s) match no failing clause` and `GET /api/services/:id/waivers` already say; the headline stays the rubric's (the effective reading is a sentence beside it). Every piece is gated on `c.waivers` being an object and every reason, author and symbol is escaped. **Inert**: a bare report renders byte for byte what it did — `tools/fixtures/golden/conformance-view/payment-service.bare.html` is the render captured at the commit before this one and `tools/test-waivers.mjs` compares the headless render to it (an array or a string in `waivers` is no block either). `tools/test-waivers.mjs` grows by two. Suite total 911 → 934 tests at this entry's commit.

### Rebadge batch 2, B3.3: diagnose → remediate flow
The response path from a firing alert to the remediation the pack declares for it, computed from pack data alone (the existing `spec.remediation` family; no external call, no store, no route, no env). The spec names a remediation's trigger as an alert id (`trigger: alert:<slug>`) and nothing else binds the two, so **`tools/lib/remediation-flow.mjs`** (new, browser-safe, vendorable — listed in `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json`; imports `artefact-model.mjs` only) is the one linking rule (`docs/ADAPTER.md` "Response path"): four tiers, the first with a hit wins and every hit of it links — the annotation `metadata.annotations["observogram.remediates.remediation[<i>]"]` (`alerting.rules[<j>]`, `policy.burn_rate_alerts[<j>]`, `slos.<id>` or `alert:<slug>`, comma-separated; a symbol naming nothing is a warning), the rule name (`slugKey(rule.name) === triggerSlug(trigger)`: lowercase, non-alphanumerics stripped, `ref:` / `alert:` dropped), a compiled burn-rule name (`<slo>_burn_<factor>x_<short>_<long>` — the compiler's formula, cross-checked against the rules it emits), the SLO. No hit is `unresolved` with suggestions (shared name tokens, the service and pack names dropped) that never link, never count and never deploy. `buildRemediationFlowModel({ pack, diff, liveAnnotations, otherSide })` → `{ configured, compared, otherSide, counts: { remediations, alerts, linked, unresolved, uncovered, blocked, suggestions, placeholder }, links[], unresolved[], uncovered[], families[], warnings[] }`; each alert's state from the comparison's buckets (`live`, `drifted`, `missing`, `unverified`, `placeholder`; `unhealthy` from the live side's `mcp.discovered.alert_rules_unhealthy`; `declared` without one), the path's state its worst alert, a Scaffold remediation (the legacy upconvert's) a `placeholder` with template values; the steps a responder walks (`deploy-alert` with the SLO's deploy action for a missing burn alert, `reconcile-alert`, `fix-alert`, `complete-alert`, `route`, `register-automation` for a URI automation or `human` for a manual one, `runbook` with an `href` for https only, `guardrails`, `annotate` for an unresolved trigger). Pure, deterministic, never throws, reads no clock. **Honest status**: not one trigger of the catalogue's 17 remediations resolves today (pinned; the named follow-up `catalogue-triggers`); a pack without `spec.remediation` is unconfigured and the model empty. `tools/test-remediation-flow.mjs` (new, 14; `npm run test:remediation-flow`, in `npm test`).
- **Studio** (`studio/remediation-flow-view.mjs`, new; the `.rflow-*` zone of `studio/ux-remediate.css`; `docs/UI_CONVENTIONS.md` §4): the **response path** panel — each remediation, the alert its trigger names with the tier that matched, that alert's state against the other side (the words follow it: `not live` / `not in the baseline`), the routes its severities reach, and the steps — drawn on Diagnose as a `.diag-block` (`#diag-flow`, a `Response path` entry in the sticky index counting blocked paths and unresolved triggers, an Open Remediate action) and on Remediate after the plan (`#rm-flow`), from one `remediationFlowHtml(model, { screen })`. The deploy button (`rflow-deploy` → `host.openDeployModal({ packId, presetIdentities })`) renders on Remediate alone, only when compared, only for a deployable SLO; a trigger no alert answers to is listed as unresolved with its name-based suggestions ("a suggestion, not a link") and the annotation to write; every operator string is escaped and a runbook is a link for `https?://` only. The engine is loaded at call time through a literal `import('/lib/remediation-flow.mjs')` (the static bundle's import map resolves it because the bundler collects literal specifiers alone; `tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` T1 pins `lib/remediation-flow.mjs` in the real graph and the view's dynamic import) and the view repaints once when it lands; a failed import is cached as null with one warning and the panel stays off. **Inert**: `packDeclaresRemediation` gates the import and the DOM, so a pack without `spec.remediation` (three catalogue packs) draws nothing; Discover, the 24 board goldens, the crawl and compile goldens are untouched. **Default-output change**: Diagnose and Remediate gain the block for packs WITH remediations (five catalogue packs, upconverted legacy packs, the typed fixture) — all-unresolved for the catalogue today. `tools/test-remediation-flow-view.mjs` (new, 10; in `npm test`): the loader, the gate, the view model, the HTML on both screens, escaping, the renderer's handler, the wiring, and the `.rflow-*` zone's tokens and WCAG AA contrast on every theme surface. Suite total 934 → 958 tests at this entry's commit.

### Rebadge batch 2, B3.4: glossary widgets
Definitions for the taxonomy's families and for spec terms, sourced from a `glossary` section of the taxonomy file (the W3 seam, schema version 2) and shown in the studio as an accessible mark beside the label they explain. Inert without a glossary: a v1 file compiles exactly as before, and every render site writes the same bytes when no entry matches.
- **`tools/lib/artefact-classify.mjs`** — the taxonomy schema is versioned: `TAXONOMY_VERSIONS = [1, 2]`, `TAXONOMY_VERSION_LATEST = 2`, and **`TAXONOMY_VERSION` keeps the value 1** (a downstream that writes `version: TAXONOMY_VERSION` keeps emitting files its deployed server accepts); the version refusal reads `taxonomy: version must be 1 or 2`. Under `version: 2` the new optional key `glossary` is an array of `{ term, definition, family?, aliases?, link? }` within `GLOSSARY_LIMITS` (`term` / alias 80, `definition` 600, `link` 2000 characters, 500 entries), each refused with an exact text (one line each; a known family, at most one entry per family; an `http(s)` link that carries no credentials — it is served to every viewer; no term or alias defined twice, case-insensitive); under `version: 1` it is `unknown key "glossary"`. The compiled object gains `glossary: { entries, byFamily, byText }` (the frozen empty glossary for a v1 file or a glossary-less v2 one) and the accessors **`glossaryFor(family)`**, **`glossaryByText(text)`** (trimmed, whitespace collapsed, case-insensitive over terms and aliases) and **`glossaryEntries()`** read the active override by default, an explicit one when given, and the empty glossary from an older compiled object (`configureTaxonomy` installs it unchanged). `classifyArtefact` reads `types` and `ids` only — a glossary changes no classification (`tools/test-artefact-classify.mjs` proves it over the fixtures and the vendored example). `describeTaxonomy` appends `, N glossary terms` only when there are some. `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json` regenerated (exports added).
- **Server** (no code change): `server/taxonomy.mjs` validates and serves the document as loaded, so a v2 file boots (`[taxonomy] loaded <path>: 7 types, 1 id rule, 6 glossary terms`) and `GET /api/taxonomy` carries the glossary verbatim; `tools/build-studio-bundle.mjs --taxonomy` bakes it like any taxonomy file (the same validator). Fixture `tools/fixtures/taxonomy/taxonomy.v2.json` (`taxonomy.json` plus six entries: four families, `Error budget`, `Criticality tier`); `taxonomy.json` stays v1 so the 24 board goldens rest on what they did. `server/fixtures/serve-child.mjs` STRIP gains the test knob `GLOSSARY_SMOKE`. Re-pins: `tools/test-artefact-classify.mjs` (the version texts), `server/test-taxonomy.mjs` (the `(+1 more)` fixture moves to `version: 3`; a v1 file with a glossary and a bad link refuse the start), `tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` (the version refusals).
- **Studio** (`studio/glossary.mjs`, new — imports `util.mjs` and `taxonomy.mjs` only; the `.ux-gloss*` zone of `studio/ux.css` beside `.ux-term`, `--ux-*` tokens only, nothing fixed or sticky; `docs/UI_CONVENTIONS.md` §4; `studio/taxonomy.mjs` gains the `typeof`-guarded passthroughs `glossaryFor`, `glossaryByText`, `familiesAt`): a **glossary mark** beside the label an entry explains — a toggletip, not a tooltip: a real `<button>` named "What is <label>?" with `aria-expanded` / `aria-controls` / `aria-describedby`, the definition `hidden` until opened (click, Enter, Space) and previewed on hover and on `:focus-visible` by CSS, Escape closing the open mark and returning the focus (swallowed only then; with no mark open the drawer's Escape is untouched), a click elsewhere closing it, a `link` as a "Learn more" anchor in a new tab; every operator string escaped. Render sites: the Discover row's kind (`artefactRowHtml`, the mark between the name button and `.dv-row-status`; the family's entry first, else the label as a term or alias; the row's click guard lets the mark keep its job), the board's group titles (`glossaryGroupMarkHtml`: the first family at home in the group that has an entry, else the title) and head facts (by label), the drawer's new *kind* row (only with an entry), its section heads (`panel()`) and field labels (`dl()`, `innerHTML` only when an entry matches, `textContent` as before otherwise); `wireGlossary(root)` on the Discover root and the drawer, once, delegated — and it returns before adding a listener when the root holds no mark. **Inert by proof**: `glossaryLabelHtml(text) === escapeHtml(text)` and every mark is `''` with no entry, so the 24 board goldens, the crawl and compile goldens and every Discover row are byte-identical with no glossary, a v1 file, or an unknown label (`tools/test-glossary.mjs`, `tools/test-discover-rows.mjs`); the one new golden `tools/fixtures/golden/board/typed.glossary.board.html` (the typed fixture under `taxonomy.v2.json`) stripped of its marks (`stripGlossaryMarks`, a balanced span walk) **is** `typed.mapped.board.html`, with the same families. The static bundle draws the same marks from a baked v2 file — B1's bake carries the document, no shim change (`tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` T8c: `/api/taxonomy` body, headers and the `--check` line `… 6 glossary terms` equal a server started with the file). **Default-output change**: none without a glossary; with one, the marks above. The Tiles, List and Cards views carry no mark (`glossary-light-views`).
- **Tests**: `tools/test-glossary.mjs` (new, 9) and `server/test-glossary-shell.mjs` (new, 1 — the real studio in headless Chromium against a v2, a v1 and an unconfigured child at 1366 and 390 px: the marks on the board and in the drawer, the keyboard toggle and the focus return, the hover preview, Escape closing a mark before the drawer, zero marks without a glossary; skipped without Playwright like `test-brand-shell.mjs`, `OBSERVOGRAM_GLOSSARY_SMOKE=require` fails instead), both in `npm test` and `npm run test:glossary`; `tools/test-artefact-classify.mjs` (four: the versions and limits, the compiled shape and unchanged classification, every refusal text, the accessors), `server/test-taxonomy.mjs` (one: a v2 file boots and is served verbatim), `tools/test-discover-rows.mjs` (one), `tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` (T8c), `tools/test-golden-board.mjs` (the glossary golden and the strip proof). ### Rebadge batch 2, PR 2: the store at schema v2 and the shared conformance refactor
- **Schema v2** (`server/store/migrations.mjs`, step `gap-batch-2`; `docs/STORE_PLAN.md` §2 "Schema v2"): a v1 database migrates at the first start of this build — two tables, `verdicts` (B3.1: a reviewer's record per artefact of a registered pack, keyed by the content-hash pack id and the adapter's positional id, cascading with the pack) and `waivers` (B3.2: a service record's time-boxed waivers of rubric findings, cascading with the service; a revoke keeps the row as history) — every v1 row intact. The default-output change this cannot avoid: PRAGMA `user_version` 1 → 2 at the first boot; `packc store backup` / `restore` print `schema v2`. **The door is one-way**: a v1 build refuses a v2 database and `packc store restore` refuses a v2 backup on a v1 build — back up before upgrading (README "Upgrade And Roll Back"); `packc store export` writes no verdict or waiver. Repositories `server/store/verdicts.mjs` (`setVerdict` / `clearVerdict` / `carryVerdicts` → audit `verdict.set`, `verdict.clear`, `verdict.carry`) and `server/store/waivers.mjs` (`createWaiver` / `revokeWaiver` → `waiver.create`, `waiver.revoke`); `requireIso` moves from `server/store/packs.mjs` to `server/store/rows.mjs` (the packs repository's exports are unchanged). `server/test-store.mjs` re-pins the schema (`V2_TABLES`, `schema v2`, the synthetic steps numbered from `SCHEMA_VERSION + 1`) and adds the real v1-file migration, the two repositories and their tenancy isolation.
- **`tools/lib/adapter.mjs`** gains the export **`overlaidCanonical(canonical, envName)`** → `{ canonical, effective }`: the env overlay applied to a copy of the whole manifest with the effective criticality / `default_target` propagated up to `metadata.bindings` — the helper `server/index.mjs` held privately since the environment overlay landed, now the adapter's so the server, the static bundle and the CLIs read one copy. The server's `GET /api/packs/:id/conformance` body is built by one function, `conformanceReportFor(meta, canonical, env)`, which the route answers and the GAP batch 2 routes read. Byte-identical: `/conformance`, `/export.zip`, every compile and deploy answer are unchanged (`server/test-smoke.mjs` and the deploy and bundle-parity suites pin the bodies).

### Rebadge batch 2, B1: bundle parity (taxonomy and brand baked into the static bundle)
The two seams a server reads from its environment — the artefact taxonomy (W3) and the brand (W4) — are baked into the static bundle (W6) through the server's own code on both sides (`docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §10, "Baking the taxonomy and the brand"). Inert by default: a build with no flags and none of the variables set produces, on the same tree, the bytes of a build with the seams unset (T4b); the goldens are untouched (4 new board goldens, none changed). No `tools/lib` module changed, so `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json` is not regenerated and no export line is due.
- **`tools/build-studio-bundle.mjs`** — `--taxonomy <file.json>` and `--brand <file.json>` (each at most once; twice or without a value exits 2 with the usage). The taxonomy file is read, parsed and validated with `validateTaxonomy` as `server/taxonomy.mjs` does, the refusals the server's texts behind the origin (`--taxonomy: <abs>: ENOENT …`, `…: invalid JSON: …`, `…: taxonomy: … (+N more)`), and the document is appended to the config as `taxonomy` — after `packs`, only when baked. The brand goes through `tools/lib/brand-env.mjs loadBrand` (the server's loader: the file, the `OBSERVOGRAM_BRAND_*` scalars on top) over a synthetic env, and the shell is `brandShellHtml(studio/index.html)` before the stylesheet inlining (`#brand-tokens` after `design-tokens.css`, the server's cascade position). When a flag is absent `OBSERVOGRAM_TAXONOMY`, `OBSERVOGRAM_BRAND_FILE` and the scalars are honoured (legacy `TOMOGRAPH_` spelling too; `main` takes `env`). A root-relative `favicon`, `logo.url` or `hero.src` is refused as a server path, the field and the fix named (`checkBrandUrls`; the default hero an unnamed brand inherits is exempt, as in every unbranded bundle). The summary line gains ` · taxonomy: N types, M id rules` / ` · brand: <name>` only when baked; `--json` gains `taxonomy` and `brand`, always present (`null` when nothing is baked), naming source, path and counts or name — never a file's contents; no operator path lands in the bundle. New exports `resolveTaxonomySource`, `loadTaxonomyFile`, `loadBundleBrand`, `checkBrandUrls`; `buildStudioBundle({ …, taxonomy, brand })` returns them (a normalized brand is used as is — normalizing it again would count its defaults as given).
- **`studio/static-backend.mjs`** (source changed — two static imports, `tools/lib/brand.mjs` and `studio/brand.mjs`, both already in the bundle graph; the bundle built from this tree therefore differs from the previous tree's in that one module's `data:` address, and in nothing else outside the import map) — `GET /api/taxonomy` answers `{ ok, taxonomy, configured }` from `config.taxonomy` with `Cache-Control: no-store` (the server's shape; unconfigured it is today's body). `denialText(feature, product)`, `noticeText(n, product)` and the new `apiMenuSubText(product)` take the product, defaulting to `DEFAULT_BRAND.name` — today's strings character for character; `installStaticBackend` reads it from the shell's `#brand-config` (a missing or malformed script is the default); `createStaticBackend(config, { …, product })` returns `product` and `taxonomyConfigured`. The file spells the product nowhere, so `tools/test-brand.mjs`'s source guard now walks it (the `EXEMPT` set is gone).
- **Fixtures and goldens**: `tools/fixtures/taxonomy/typed-canonical.pack.json` (`examples/production-curated.pack.yaml` as JSON with seven declared types — a canonical pack `--pack` and the server both accept) and `tools/fixtures/brand/acme-static.json` (acme with bundle-safe URLs: inline SVG logo, `data:` favicon, no hero; `acme.json` stays the server fixture and is the bundle's refusal case). `tools/test-golden-board.mjs` renders the new fixture unmapped and mapped — 4 new goldens, none changed — and exports `boardGroupCounts`.
- **Tests**: `tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs` 9 → 13 (T4b inert proof, T8 taxonomy parity against a configured server and the mapped golden, T8b brand parity against a branded server's shell, T9 the baked bundle in headless Chromium — skips like T7); every CLI spawn now gets serve-child's stripped env. Suite total 843 → 847.
- Docs: README "Serve The Studio Without The Server" (*Bake the seams*), "Rebadge The Studio" and "Classify Typed Packs"; `docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §9 rows and §10 (the Compare/lezer deferral with its measured blockers); `docs/UPDATE_JOURNEY.md` decisions; `docs/VENDORING.md`, `docs/UI_CONVENTIONS.md`, `docs/ADAPTER.md`, `.env.example`.

### Rebadge batch 2, B2b: a fresh crawl is a valid pack, and it says what it invented
The crawler validated its own output and, when a repository's folder name, a rule name, a group interval, a dashboard uid or schemaVersion or an alert-derived id fell outside the spec, printed the pack anyway with *this is a crawler bug* and exit 3; the server registered nothing and the studio's folder scan failed on any folder with a capital letter. A fresh crawl now validates or exits 3 for a real bug, and the flags the spec cannot hold are refused before the crawl (`docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §12).
- **Crawler** (`tools/lib/crawler.mjs`) — names the manifest spells as spec Slugs are normalized with the original kept (`metadata.name` / `bindings.service` from the folder name, `crawler.nameNormalizedFrom`; the environment, `crawler.environmentNormalizedFrom`; owners, in `summary.normalized.owners` only — an owner string may be an address; a digit-led name is prefixed `svc-`/`env-`/`owner-`, never truncated at the front); `criticality` / `binding` outside the spec enums are defaulted with a warning and recorded in `crawler.criticalityIgnored` / `crawler.bindingIgnored`; recording rules not named `<service>:<metric>:<op>` are recorded in `crawler.omitted.recording_rules` (+ `crawler.omittedRecordingRuleCount`, `summary.omitted.recordingRules`) instead of declared — the reading the live fetcher already applied — while their expressions still feed the metric inventory; the Grafana unified-alerting recording form (`record: { metric, from }`, the expression in `data[].model.expr`) is read; a numeric rule-group interval becomes a Duration (`30` → `30s`), a string is kept verbatim when it is one and recorded in `summary.omitted.ruleIntervals` when it is not; dashboards: the id is the uid, else the title, trailing separators trimmed after the cut and `d-` in front when not letter-led; a `schemaVersion` below 30 is not declared but kept in `params.schema_version` with a warning; tier-3 fallback SLI/SLO ids are letter-led Slugs of at most 64 characters; the stub dashboard and synthetic-check ids fit 64 characters; `otlphttp` exporters are `otlp` (the alternation was wrong: they read `prometheusremotewrite`); a `server/` folder no longer names a project-specific service and a Java file under `event/` no longer gets a product-specific metric prefix (the two non-validity output changes of the commit). `summary.normalized` and `summary.discovered.recordingRulesOmitted` are new keys, always present. Golden crawl byte-identical.
- **CLI** (`tools/crawl-repo.mjs`) — `--name`, `--env`, each `--owners` entry, `--criticality` and `--binding` are validated against the vendored schema before the crawl: exit 2 with the rule in the message (`a spec Slug: 2-64 lowercase letters, digits, '_' or '-', starting with a letter and ending with a letter or digit`; the enum values for the two vocabularies), nothing on stdout; a dangling `--owners` is a usage error, not a stack trace; two new summary lines (`name : <name> (normalized from '<raw>')`, `rules omitted : N` when non-zero); the exit-code table in `--help` (0 valid · 2 usage · 3 schema — a crawler bug). The folder-name default is not validated: the library normalizes it and the summary says so.
- **Server** — `POST /api/crawl-github`'s own copy of the Slug rule (which could still yield a digit-led or `-`-terminated name) is gone; the raw `owner-repo` goes to the library, one rule for the three doors.
- **Libraries** — `tools/lib/slug.mjs` gains **`packSlug(s, fallback = 'crawled-service', { prefix = 'svc-' })`** (the spec Slug flavour beside `fileSlug` / `serviceSlug` / `symbolSlug`); `tools/lib/sli-inference.mjs` gains **`isSpecRecordingRuleName(name)`** (the one `<service>:<metric>:<op>` reading, now shared by `tools/fetch-live-pack.mjs`, which drops its local copy) and **`SPEC_DURATION_RE`** (the schema's `$defs.Duration` pattern, mirrored; the new test pins the mirror). `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json` regenerated.
- **Provenance** (`tools/lib/crawler.mjs`, `tools/lib/alert-routes.mjs`) — every value the crawler invents is marked `crawler.scaffold.<symbol>` with a one-line reason, in the one marker vocabulary B2a reads (`docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §11.1): the stub SLI/SLO pair and every tier-3 alert-derived pair at the ARTEFACT level (`slis.<id>`, `slos.<id>` — the adapter parks them as Scaffold and Compare files them in its scaffold bucket, the reading the live fetcher already applies to its own `platform_*` stubs), and at the FIELD level — parking nothing in the studio or in Compare — `metadata.owners` when the default `team-platform` is used, the five `otel.*` fields (`semconv`, `resource_attributes`, `sdk.languages`, `sdk.sampling`, `sdk.propagators`; an artefact-level `otel` mark would move OTEL-01 out of Compare's not-observed bucket), `telemetry.backends.<id>.endpoints` when the port was assumed (`http://<product>:80`), and `alerting.routes[i].channels[j]` for every address the Alertmanager reading had to invent (collected by position through the new `invented` collector of `routesFromAlertmanagerConfig` / `receiverChannels`; values unchanged, the live fetcher passes none). `summary.invented = { endpoints, channels }` is a new key; `summary.scaffold` and `crawler.scaffoldCount` count the marks. Dashboards: `provider.version` is the Grafana image tag the repository deploys (compose/Helm), or absent — never the dashboard's own revision counter (`"1"`), so `compile.mjs` resolves a real profile; a dashboard that states no `schemaVersion` declares none (the `41` default is gone); two files with the same uid are declared as `<id>`, `<id>-2` with a warning.
- **`otel.sdk.languages` from the repository** (`tools/lib/crawler.mjs`) — the source files (test paths aside) name the SDK languages: `.go` → go, `.java`/`.kt` → java, `.py` → python, `.js`/`.mjs`/`.cjs`/`.jsx`/`.ts`/`.tsx` → node, `.rs` → rust, `.cs` → dotnet, sorted and unique, recorded in `crawler.discovered.sdk_languages`; only a repository with no source files keeps the `['go']` default, marked `crawler.scaffold.otel.sdk.languages`. A heuristic over extensions (a Go service with a `scripts/*.js` helper reports both); the operator edits the pack.
- **Goldens** — `tools/fixtures/golden-crawl.pack.json` regenerated twice, each diff stated. The provenance commit: `crawler.scaffoldCount` `4` → `10`; six annotation keys appended after `crawler.scaffold.validation.synthetic_checks.golden-health-canary` (`crawler.scaffold.metadata.owners`, `crawler.scaffold.otel.semconv`, `…otel.resource_attributes`, `…otel.sdk.languages`, `…otel.sdk.sampling`, `…otel.sdk.propagators`); `spec.dashboards[0].provider.version` `"1"` → `"12.0.0"` (the compose `grafana/grafana:12.0.0` tag; the key now follows `schemaVersion`). Nothing else moves. The languages commit: `spec.otel.sdk.languages` `["go"]` → `["node"]` (from `src/metrics.ts`), `crawler.scaffold.otel.sdk.languages` removed, `crawler.scaffoldCount` `10` → `9`, `crawler.discovered.sdk_languages: ["node"]` added. The validity commit left it byte-identical.
- **Tests** — `tools/test-crawl-canonical.mjs` (in `npm test` and `npm run test:crawl:canonical`) over two fixture repositories on disk (`tools/fixtures/crawl/canonical/Hostile_Repo` with every hostile value, `tier3-alerts` with alerts only, `helm-dashboard` with a ConfigMap dashboard stating no schemaVersion): validity, the normalizations, the omitted rules and their evidence, the Grafana record form, the dashboard ids and `params.schema_version`, the exporter fix, no project vocabulary, the tier-3 ids, the mirror pins (`SPEC_DURATION_RE` = `$defs.Duration`, every `packSlug` result validates and a valid input is a fixed point, every Binding / Criticality echoes and an unknown one defaults), the CLI's end-to-end door (crawl → validate-pack) and its refusals; the provenance marks by symbol (field marks flip nothing for the adapter, the tier-3 pairs are Scaffold and land in Compare's scaffold bucket, never `onlyInA`), the field/artefact partition, the dashboard dedupe and `provider.version`. `tools/test-crawl.mjs` pins that an assumed port marks the endpoints field only and that `otel` is marked field by field; `tools/test-pack-conformance.mjs` re-pins the crawler's rows (an empty crawl: 17 marks, the version its one unmarked row).

### Rebadge batch 2, B2a: canonical pack production — pack conformance and a merge-safe upconvert
The paved road from "validates" to "conformant" (`docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §11): a pack's placeholders are the artefacts whose adapter symbol carries a scaffold marker, and one tool lists them with the pack path, the field, what it needs and where the value normally comes from.
- **`tools/lib/pack-conformance.mjs`** (new, zero-import, browser-safe, vendorable — listed in `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json`, 51 modules) — `packConformance(canonical)` → `{ name, writers, markers, rows, counts, conformant }`, one row `{ path, symbol, field, needs, source, hint, state, marker, writer, rule }` per field that still has to become real; `scaffoldMarkers(canonical)` (the three prefixes with a non-empty string value — the adapter's own test, so the report is what the studio parks as Scaffold; a library-style `<field>: <what> · …` value names its own fields and literal), `parseSymbol` / `resolveSymbol` (the adapter's symbol grammar, inverse of `library.mjs` `symbolOf`, with FIELD symbols — `otel.semconv`, `telemetry.backends.<id>.endpoints`, `alerting.routes[i].channels[j]`, `metadata.owners` — as `rest`), `SYMBOL_FAMILIES` (report order and the merge identity per family), `PLACEHOLDER_FIELDS` (family → fields with `needs`, `source`, `hint`, the upstream stub literal test and the `placeholder.<family>.<field>` rule), `SCAFFOLD_PREFIXES` (a text-pinned copy of `adapter.mjs`'s, never an import: the adapter is in the static bundle's module graph), `SOURCES` (`crawl` · `operator` · `telemetry`), `STATES` (`placeholder` · `marker-only` · `unmarked` · `dangling`). The crawler-default fingerprints (`0.1.0-crawled`, `['team-platform']`) are reported `unmarked` only on a crawler-written pack, so every shipped catalogue pack reports zero rows.
- **`tools/pack-conformance.mjs`** (new; `npm run pack-conformance -- <pack.yaml|pack.json> [...] [--json] [--strict] [--quiet]`, `packc conformance`) — the schema check first (a legacy layered pack is refused with the upconvert command, an invalid pack with the validator's; never auto-converted, so every row points into a file the operator can edit), then one `✗`/`✓` headline per pack (`N placeholder artefact(s), M field(s) need real values  [spec vX · <writers> · K marker(s)]`), the rows grouped by artefact with the field, the source and what it needs, one tail line per state (`-> then delete metadata.annotations["<marker>"]` / `the value no longer matches an upstream stub` / `no marker: the value is an upstream stub literal` / `it names no artefact`), the by-source / by-state line and one rubric line (`rubric @ tier: MUST a/b · score n%` — the rubric grades what is declared, placeholders included; the rows are what still has to become real). Exit 0 every pack read, canonical and valid (rows are informational); 1 unreadable / previous-format / invalid, or `--strict` and any pack has rows (ANY state: a marker-only artefact is still parked Scaffold, a dangling marker is a broken contract); 2 usage. `--json`: `{ tool, specVersion, strict, packs: [{ path, valid, errors, rubric, ...report }], totals, exitCode }`, errors still on stderr. `--quiet`: headlines and the counts line only. Every version string derives from `SPEC_VERSION`; the schema resolves through `fileURLToPath`.
- **`tools/lib/legacy.mjs`** — `upconvertLegacyPack` returns a third key, `provenance` (symbol → the `legacy.artefact.<LAYER>.<ID>` record of the legacy item it maps to, `null` for a schema-required stub; `report`'s shape is unchanged, the server gate still destructures `{ canonical, report }`). New export **`mergeUpconvert({ canonical, provenance }, existing)`** → `{ canonical, report: { kept, added, skipped, scaffoldCount, danglingRefs } }`: the existing pack wins for every artefact it has (by identity: `id`, `name`, `ref`, `slo`, `trigger`, `severity#k`, the storage/exporter key), the upconvert only adds artefacts whose legacy record the existing pack has never seen (a deleted artefact stays deleted; a schema-required stub is skipped), added items land at the end of their list with their marker and type annotation re-indexed to the final position; existing annotations keep their order and values, the whole `legacy.*` block is refreshed (records, `legacy.upconvertedAt`), `legacy.scaffoldCount` is recounted. Pure, idempotent; throws on a non-canonical base. The module now imports `tools/lib/pack-conformance.mjs` (`SYMBOL_FAMILIES`): a downstream that vendored `legacy.mjs` alone copies the two together (the manifest's `imports` says so).
- **`tools/upconvert-legacy.mjs`** — idempotent and merge-safe (SPEC B2). **Default-behaviour changes**: a canonical input (apiVersion/kind) is validated and passed through unchanged with exit 0 (was exit 1 "not a legacy layered pack"); `-o` onto an existing canonical file now **merges** into it (was an unconditional overwrite) — `--overwrite` restores the plain write, nothing restores the old exit 1. New flags `--merge <existing.pack.json|yaml>` (an explicit base; without `-o` the merged pack goes to stdout) and `--overwrite`; the input and the base may be JSON or YAML (validate-pack's loader); an unreadable, non-canonical or schema-invalid base is refused with exit 1 (`refusing to merge; pass --overwrite`), never clobbered, and so is an existing `-o` file that is not the `--merge` base (`exists and is not the merge base`). stderr: `merged <in> into <base>: kept N artefact(s) from <base>, added A new, skipped S (removed before, or schema-required stubs); K scaffold marker(s) remain`; the fresh-convert line now points at `node tools/pack-conformance.mjs`. The schema resolves through `fileURLToPath` for uniformity with the other CLIs (the previous `new URL(…)` read was already win32-safe).
- **Tests** — `tools/test-pack-conformance.mjs` (9 tests at this entry's commit, 14 at the head of the branch — B2b's hostile crawl, the review fixes' phone fingerprint, the flag-documentation pin and GAP batch 2's two `--waivers` cases, counted in their own entries: the vocabularies and the adapter-pinned prefixes, the symbol grammar round-trip through `library.mjs` `symbolOf` over every writer's packs, the four legacy examples with pinned counts and the "every marker row is `placeholder`" stub-table drift guard, the state transitions, the crawler's unmarked stub, the library note shapes, the catalogue pin — every shipped canonical pack reports zero rows, krystaline pins 3 markers + 2 unmarked crawler-default rows — the fetcher's stubs, the CLI's exit codes / `--json` / `--strict` / `--quiet`, and the inert proof over the static bundle's module graph) and `tools/test-upconvert-merge.mjs` (8 tests: the count pins and the annotation key order, provenance, pass-through idempotency, the merge scenario, the never-regresses property over the four examples, the refusal, the CLI merge paths, determinism); both in `npm test` and `npm run test:pack-conformance`. `tools/test-legacy-pack.mjs` pins `report.scaffolded` to the marker count.
- **`tools/lib/legacy.mjs`** — the six shared-section markers (`crawler.scaffold.otel`, `pipelines.receivers[0]`, `pipelines.processors[0]`, `pipelines.exporters.{metrics,logs,traces}`) now ride in `report.scaffolded` and `legacy.scaffoldCount` (they were written outside the count): demo-skeleton 9 → 15, production-curated 27 → 33, production-live 29 → 35, target-advanced 39 → 45. **The one deliberate default-output change of this item**: the value of the `legacy.scaffoldCount` annotation (one byte run; the annotation key order is unchanged — the six keys stay last), `report.scaffolded` in the `POST /api/validate` response and the studio's upload toast. No golden covers the upconvert; `tools/test-legacy-pack.mjs` now pins the count to the key count.

### Rebadge batch 2, B4: Windows portability
`npm test` on Windows: expected green except the explicit, counted skips (README "Platforms"; `docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §13) — predicted from code reading, no Windows run exists yet; the downstream's first `npm test` on Windows is the acceptance. Tests, fixtures and docs only — no module under `tools/lib`, `server/` runtime or `studio/` changes, so `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json` needs no regeneration and no manifest line, and every golden is byte-identical.
- **Fixed, `URL.pathname` as a filesystem path**: `tools/test-brand.mjs` and `tools/test-crawl-alerting-rules.mjs` resolved their root from `new URL(…, import.meta.url).pathname`, which on Windows is `/C:/repo/…` — `path.win32` treats it as drive-relative and the suites died at import reading `C:\C:\repo\…`; the same expression is percent-encoded on every platform (a checkout under `Jane Doe/` read `Jane%20Doe`). Both now `fileURLToPath(new URL(…, import.meta.url))`, as does `tools/test-deploy-manifests.mjs`, whose hand-rolled drive strip (`pathname.replace(/^\/([A-Za-z]:)/, '$1')`) kept the `%20`.
- **Fixed, the studio-bundle T1 missing-module assertion** (`tools/test-studio-bundle.mjs`): the ENOENT text names the host path, so its separator is normalised (`split(sep).join('/')`) before the `tools/lib/does-not-exist.mjs` match; byte-identical behaviour on Linux.
- **Fixed, `closeStore()` before every workspace removal** in the five in-process suites that `rmSync` a directory holding their own open sqlite file — `server/test-auth-local.mjs`, `test-auth-oidc.mjs`, `test-tenancy.mjs`, `test-smoke.mjs`, `test-workspace.mjs` (`start()` never closes the store on `srv.close`, and the default store lives inside the workspace). Windows cannot unlink a file another handle holds open (EBUSY/EPERM); on Linux the close only moves earlier, and a use-after-close would throw `ERR_OBSERVOGRAM_STORE_NOT_OPEN` at once.
- **`server/fixtures/platform.mjs`** (new; imports nothing) — `isWin32`, `win32Skip(reason)` (node:test's option form: `false` runs, a string skips with `win32: <reason>`), `skipOnWin32(t, reason)` (mid-suite; `t.skip(…)` or, for a harness suite with `t = null`, a printed `- SKIP win32: <reason>` line), `isLinux`, `PLATFORM` (for a printout naming the host), `platformHelpers(platform, out)` (injectable, for the guard) and the three reasons `WIN32.modes` ("POSIX mode bits: Windows reports 0666/0444 and chmod cannot make a file unreadable"), `WIN32.signals` ("signal semantics: process.kill(pid, "SIGTERM"|"SIGINT") ends a Windows process outright — no handler runs, the store cannot close itself"), `WIN32.symlinks` ("symlinks: creating one needs a privilege (Developer Mode or an elevated shell) on Windows"). The one place a suite names win32.
- **The reasoned skips, 15 sites, never a silent pass**: `server/test-store.mjs` (8 — the `child()` deadline test, the SIGTERM/SIGINT close loop, the own-handler loop and the dropped re-raise loop: signals; the backup-refusal test's symlinked-directory paragraph, now a subtest; the restore-while-held test, with its own reason — the holder is ended by TerminateProcess, so its `-wal`/`-shm` survive and the final directory listing differs; the two 0600/mode tests), `server/test-store-ops.mjs` (4 — the three existing `process.platform === 'win32' && '…'` options re-spelled through the fixture; purge-org's symlinked-root paragraph, now a subtest), `server/test-store-import.mjs` (2 — the EACCES reader test; `hasData`'s symlink cases split into their own test), `tools/test-journey.mjs` (1 — the silent `if (process.platform !== 'win32')` around the symlinked-file crawl now prints the `- SKIP` line). A POSIX paragraph inside a portable test becomes `await t.test(…, { skip: win32Skip(…) })`, never a mid-test `t.skip` after assertions ran. Expected on Windows: 19 skipped tests once the loops unroll — 19 `SKIP win32:` lines (18 `# SKIP win32:` from node:test, one `- SKIP win32:` from test-journey) — plus the PID 1 namespace test's `# SKIP unshare --pid is unavailable here` and the browser suites' Playwright skips when `OBSERVOGRAM_PLAYWRIGHT` is unset; an elevated runner (GitHub `windows-latest`) can create symlinks and sees the 4 symlink skips as tests it could run. The flat-export `index.json` mode expectation reads 0666 on Windows (a writable file), 0644 elsewhere; `tools/test-backend-validate.mjs` reads `isWin32` for the `.exe` suffix and the mimirtool notice and `PLATFORM` for its text (not a skip site); `test-store.mjs`'s `unshare` probe reads `isLinux`, and its redundant linux guard around the real-tempdir `checkFilesystem` assertion is dropped (`unchecked` off linux).
- **`tools/test-platform.mjs`** (new, in `npm test`; `npm run test:platform`) — six Linux-runnable proofs: P1 the resolver fed a `file:///C:/Users/Jane%20Doe/…` URL (`URL.pathname` drive-relative and encoded, `fileURLToPath(…, { windows: true })` right; the three repaired resolvers on this host), P2 the T1 separator idiom and the site, P3 the fixture with the platform injected (win32 skips with the reason, linux runs silently), P4 every `.pathname` under `server/` and `tools/` (tokenizer-blanked code) is in the pinned allowlist of known URL paths — `server/store/identity.mjs`, `server/test-auth-oidc.mjs`, `tools/build-studio-bundle.mjs`, `tools/record-mcp-fixtures.mjs` — so a filesystem use in any form (the two-step `const u = new URL(…); u.pathname` included) is reported file:line, P5 no suite or fixture but the platform fixture reads `process.platform` or spells `'win32'`, P6 every `win32Skip`/`skipOnWin32` argument a reason (fails closed: a reason with `)` or not single-quoted is reported), `isWin32` a data read in its two allowed places only and never inside `if (…)` or a `skip:` option, and README's "15 win32-skip sites" plus the per-file counts equal to the sources — a new skip site without its README bump fails the build. Verified here by a run of the three store suites with the fixture's platform faked to win32: exactly the 18 `# SKIP win32:` lines, every other test green but the one `isWin32` data read (the 0666 expectation against a real 0644 file — the fake's artefact).
- **`.gitattributes`**: `* text=auto eol=lf` as the first rule — `*.css`, `*.svg` and `*.txt` were outside the explicit list; the index held 0 CRLF entries, so no renormalisation.
- Docs: README "Platforms" (under Quickstart's Node paragraph) and the Repository Map rows (`server/fixtures/`, `tools/test-platform.mjs`); `docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §13 "Platforms" and §8; `docs/UPDATE_JOURNEY.md` B4 paragraph. Deferred (DOWNSTREAM §13): the Windows CI leg, to land after the downstream's first Windows run confirms the count. 866 → 875 tests.
- **Tests**: `tools/test-doc-test-totals.mjs` (new, in `npm test`) — the suite totals these documents quote chain: within a section of `docs/UPDATE_JOURNEY.md` every `Tests:` note starts where the previous entry ends, and a pair quoted under `## Unreleased` here starts from one total only and agrees with the journey's (the batch's items had each quoted the total measured alone on its parent — `843 → 847`, `843 → 852` — which no one branch can have). The batch's chain, measured per commit: 843 → 847 (B1) → 864 (B2a) → 866 (B2b) → 875 (B4), then 875 → 880 tests with the review fixes (two in `tools/test-upconvert-merge.mjs`) and this guard (three). The guard also reads the batch's delivery report, **`docs/DELIVERY-REBADGE-BATCH2.md`** (new — the acceptance's "short delivery report": per work item what shipped, the test counts, what is deferred and why; B3 is the second PR), which must exist and quote only pairs the journey states: 880 → 881 tests. A later review fix landed one test in `tools/test-pack-conformance.mjs` (the unmarked `+0-000-` phone fingerprint) without its note, so the guard also sums, for every flat suite this batch added, the counts the journey narrates against the `test(` calls the file holds: 881 → 883 tests. `packc --help` now lists `--quiet` for `conformance` (the tool took it unadvertised); `tools/test-pack-conformance.mjs` keeps every flag the CLI parses in its usage, the `packc` help line, the README synopsis and this entry: 883 → 884 tests. The B2a Tests entry above still said `tools/test-pack-conformance.mjs` held 11 at the head of the branch after that pin made it 12 (the phrase is not a `Tests: a → b` pair, so no chain check read it); the guard now holds every such head-of-branch count a document states for a flat suite to the `test(` calls the file holds: 884 → 885 tests.

### Downstream seams, W4: the rebadge pack (one brand config)
The branding seam (`docs/DOWNSTREAM.md` §9): one brand object feeds the studio shell, the JS chrome, the auth pages and the design tokens; a downstream rebadges with a JSON file or a handful of env scalars, and one `name` is enough. Inert by default: with nothing configured every renderer answers today's strings and the shell is served byte-identical (`tools/test-brand.mjs`, `server/test-brand-shell.mjs` prove it).
- **`tools/lib/brand.mjs`** (new, zero-import, browser-safe, vendorable — listed in `VENDOR-MANIFEST.json`, 50 modules) — `DEFAULT_BRAND` (= `normalizeBrand({})`, today's Observogram string for string, frozen), `normalizeBrand(raw)` (every field defaulted; the defaults derive from `name` when the override names it — `shortName`, the wordmark, the scanner title, the compass mark, the footer text, the hero alt, the description, the footer links and the About changelog link — and are the upstream literals when it does not; `configured` = anything given; refuses a token name outside `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$`, a token value carrying `;{}<>`, a `logo.svg` that is not `<svg…` or carries `<script`), `brandChrome(brand)` (every chrome string, functions for the wordmark/logo/version tooltip; everything escaped where it lands, `logo.svg` the one raw field), `brandTokensCss`, `brandShellHtml(html, brand)` (the identity when unconfigured; otherwise seven exact anchors on `studio/index.html` — title, meta description, legacy h1 and tagline, footer text before `#build-label` (kept, with its version), footer links, and after the design-tokens link the `#brand-tokens` style, the `#brand-config` JSON and the favicon — a missing anchor throws), `brandConfigScript`, `SHELL_ANCHORS`, `SPEC_LINK`, `DEFAULT_LOGO_SVG`, `TOKEN_NAME_RE`, `escapeHtml`.
- **`tools/lib/brand-env.mjs`** — `loadBrand({ env = process.env })`: the JSON file `OBSERVOGRAM_BRAND_FILE` names (resolved against the working directory), then the scalars `OBSERVOGRAM_BRAND_NAME` / `_SHORT_NAME` / `_TAGLINE` / `_LOGO_URL` / `_DOCS_URL` / `_FOOTER` / `_ACCENT` / `_ACCENT_DARK` on top (`TOMOGRAPH_*` honoured); an unreadable or invalid file throws `brand file <path>: not found | is a directory | unreadable | not valid JSON | not a JSON object` — never the contents. Cached per process for the default env (`resetBrandCache()`); `brandSource(env)` (the path, `'env'` or `null`), `BRAND_ENV` (the nine suffixes).
- **Server** (`server/index.mjs`): two new public routes, **`GET /`** and **`GET /index.html`**, registered before the studio static mount, plus the SPA fallback — one `sendShell`: unbranded it is `res.sendFile(studio/index.html)` (the same `send` pipeline the static mount ran, so the bytes and the headers — `ETag`, `Last-Modified`, `Cache-Control: public, max-age=0`, `Accept-Ranges` — are the ones `/` answered before, and a conditional GET still 304s); branded it is `brandShellHtml`'s rendering held in memory, `Cache-Control: no-cache`. The static mount drops `index: 'index.html'` and `extensions: ['html']` (`index: false`), so `GET /index` — which used to reach the on-disk file around any handler — now falls to the SPA fallback like every other path (same bytes in default mode; the branded shell in a branded one; `studio/` holds one `.html`, so nothing else changes). The brand is read in `start()` (`loadBrand()`, before the store opens: an unreadable or invalid file refuses the start with `brand file <path>: <reason>`), logged once as `[studio] brand: <name> (<path> | OBSERVOGRAM_BRAND_* env)`. `server/route-table.mjs` gains both rows; `server/test-authz.mjs` EXPECTED_CLASS and the README API Surface intro and rows name them.
- **Auth pages** (`server/auth.mjs` `loginPageHtml`, `changePageHtml`, `authPageChrome`; `server/auth-proxy.mjs` `explainerPageHtml`, all exported): title, wordmark and subtitle from the brand, read at request time through the cached loader; error texts and fields unchanged.
